-----------------------------------------------------
DataBank: 00

$1E00 - [AKAO_COMMAND]
$1E01 - [AKAO_ARGUMENT1]
$1E02 - [AKAO_ARGUMENT2]
$1E03 - [AKAO_ARGUMENT3]

$1E05 - [CURRENT_SONG] - Id of the song that is currently playing. 0xFF if nothing is playing.

$1E09 - [PREV_SONG]
$1E0A - Set to $1E06 when $1E05 is not negative.

$1E10 - [AKAO_PROGRAM_SIZE (Low)]
$1E11 - [AKAO_PROGRAM_SIZE (High)]

$1E14 - [LOAD_AKAO_PROGRAM_ADDRESS (Low)]
$1E15 - [LOAD_AKAO_PROGRAM_ADDRESS (High)]
$1E16 - [LOAD_AKAO_PROGRAM_ADDRESS (Bank)]

$1EEA - [SONG_SEQ_BASE_OFFSET] - Offset into the AkaoSongSequencePointerTable for the current song.

$1E06 - Command01: ([AKAO_ARGUMENT2] & 0x0F) is stored here.
$1E07 - Command01: [AKAO_ARGUMENT3] is stored here.

$1E28 - An array
$1E47 - Above Array End
$1E48 - 0x4DF1 gets stored here at Init

$1E4B - An array
$1E6A - Above Array End

$1EF0 - 0x02 gets stored here at Init.
      - Seems to be the current EchoBufferSize of the APU.

$1EF1 - 0xEA gets stored here at Init.
$1EF2 - 0xEA gets stored here at Init.

$1EF8 - 0x4DF1 gets stored here at Init.
$1EFA - 0x00 gets stored here at Init
$1EFB - 0xC3 gets stored here at Init

$1E28-$1E47 - Loaded BRR Samples Array
$1E8A-$1EA9 - Matched BRR Samples Array
$1ECA-$1EE9 - BRR Sample Index To Load Array

;2D = C3/44C2

$2143 - [APUIO3]
$2142 - [APUIO2]
$2141 - [APUIO1]
$2140 - [APUIO0]

$12 = $05A0
$14 = $1EAA
$16 = $1EDA
------------------------------------------

; Startup commands from C7 RAM block.
; 01 2D 0B 0F
; 01 2C 0F 0F

[InitalizeAkaoSPCEngineTrampoline]
;     Entry point for the RAM boot-loader.
030000: 4C2C00          JMP $002C       ;Jump to InitalizeAkaoSPCEngine.
030003: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (What? Why is this here?)

[RunAkaoCommandTrampoline]
;     Entry point for all sound commands from any bank.
030004: 4C6001          JMP $0160       ;Jump to RunAkaoCommand.
030007: EA              NOP             ;No Operation. (What? Why is this here?)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[AkaoSPCProgramPointerTable]
C3/0008:	4807 [00: AkaoSPCEngineAssemblyCode]
C3/000A:	3721 [01: AkaoSoundFXSequenceData]
C3/000C:	E21A [02: SoundFXBitRateReductionSampleData]
C3/000E:	D520 [03: SoundFXSampleSourceDirectory]
C3/0010:	0321 [04: AkaoADSRDefaultValueTable]
C3/0012:	1D21 [05: ]

[AkaoSPCProgramStartAddressTable]
C3/0014:	0002 [00: AkaoSPCEngineAssemblyCode]
C3/0016:	002C [01: AkaoSoundFXSequenceData]
C3/0016:	0048 [02: SoundFXBitRateReductionSampleData]
C3/001A:	0019 [03: SoundFXSampleSourceDirectory]
C3/001C:	8018 [04: AkaoADSRDefaultValueTable]
C3/001E:	0018 [05: ]

[More pointers?]
; This pointer table seems unused, but all the tables it points to are.
C3/0020:	393D [00: AkaoSongSequencePointerTable]  - Song_Data_Pointers             ; Pointers tp Song Data 
C3/0022:	F93D [01: BRRSamplesPointerTable]        - Instrument_BRR_Pointers        ; Pointers to Instrument BRR Data
C3/0022:	5C3E [02: Some kind off offset table]    - Instrument_Loop_Start_Data     ; Instrument Loop Start Data
C3/0026:	E03E [03: Strange data table]            - Instrument_ADSR_Data           ; Instrument ADSR Data
C3/0028:	9E3E [04: Another strangle data table]   - Instrument_Wave_Rate_Data      ; Instrument Wave Rate Data
C3/002A:	223F [05: AkaoSoundTrackInstrumentTable] - Instruments_for_each_Song      ; Instruments for Each Song

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[InitalizeAkaoSPCEngine]
C3/002C:	8B              PHB             ;Push Data Bank Register.
C3/002D:	0B              PHD             ;Push Direct Page Register.
C3/002E:	08              PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
C3/002F:	C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/0031:	C210            REP #$10        ;Enable 16-Bit Index Registers. (Sure, do it the hard way)
C3/0033:	48              PHA             ;Push Accumulator.
C3/0034:	DA              PHX             ;Push Register X.
C3/0035:	5A              PHY             ;Push Register Y.
C3/0036:	E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/0038:	A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into the Accumulator.
C3/003A:	48              PHA             ;Push 0x00 into the stack.
C3/003B:	AB              PLB             ;Pull 0x00 into Data Bank Register.
C3/003C:	A2001E          LDX #$1E00      ;Load 0x1E00 into Register X. (Range for music variables)
C3/003F:	DA              PHX             ;Push the music variable offset into the stack.
C3/0040:	2B              PLD             ;Pull the music variable offset into the Direct Page Register.
C3/0041:	A2AABB          LDX #$BBAA      ;0xBBAA is the Magic Number that the SPC700 APU will send when it is ready to be programmed.
C3/0044:	A00008          LDY #$0800      ;Load 0x0800 into Register Y. (Maximum number of SpinWait iterations to wait on the APU to initialize)

[SPC700InitalizeTimedSpinWait]
C3/0047:	EC4021          CPX $2140       ;Is the value of [APUIO0] 0xBBAA yet?
C3/004A:	F032            BEQ $007E       ;Branch to SPC700InitalizeWait if the APU has finished booting.
C3/004C:	88              DEY             ;Decrement Register Y.
C3/004D:	D0F8            BNE $0047       ;Branch back to SPC700InitalizeTimedSpinWait.

; What is all this crap? If the busy loop runs out before the SPC700 is initialized you say fuck it and try to play music anyway?
; Well I guess since it's not programmed yet and it's not the one command it listens for the writes are ignored.
; The legion of conditions makes it seem like its impossible for this to occur, but if it did it would start an SPC program before we loaded it
; and before the chip even finished initializing. If it hadn't crashed at that point then it trys to tell the SPC to play something.
; Maybe this is for a Reset? SPC initializes faster or something.
C3/004F:	A4F8            LDY $F8         ;[00:1EF8] (0xF1 at start up)
C3/0051:	F02B            BEQ $007E       ;SPC700InitalizeSpinWait
C3/0053:	C448            CPY $48         ;[00:1E48] (0xF1 at start up)
C3/0055:	D027            BNE $007E       ;SPC700InitalizeSpinWait
C3/0057:	A9F0            LDA #$F0        ;Load 0xF0 into the Accumulator.
C3/0059:	C500            CMP $00         ;[00:1E00] (0xF0 at start up)
C3/005B:	D021            BNE $007E       ;SPC700InitalizeSpinWait
C3/005D:	A908            LDA #$08        ;Load 0x08 into the Accumulator.
C3/005F:	8D4121          STA $2141       ;[APUIO1]
C3/0062:	A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into the Accumulator.
C3/0064:	8D4021          STA $2140       ;[APUIO0]
C3/0067:	A2F000          LDX #$00F0

C3/006A:	9DFF1D          STA $1DFF,X     ;Store 0x00 into $1E00-$1EEF. (So all but the last 16 bytes of the APU memory page?)
C3/006D:	CA              DEX 
C3/006E:	D0FA            BNE $006A

C3/0070:	A4F8            LDY $F8         ;[00:1EF8] (0xF1 at start up)
C3/0072:	8448            STY $48         ;[00:1E48] (0xF1 at start up)
C3/0074:	3A              DEC A
C3/0075:	8505            STA $05         ;[00:1E05]
C3/0077:	A9F0            LDA #$F0
C3/0079:	8500            STA $00         ;[00:1E00]
C3/007B:	4C7501          JMP $0175

[SPC700InitalizeSpinWait]
;     SNES CPU will do nothing but spin until the APU is initialized.
C3/007E:	EC4021          CPX $2140       ;Is the value of [APUIO0] 0xBBAA yet?
C3/0081:	D0FB            BNE $007E       ;Branch back to SPC700InitalizeSpinWait if not.

[SPC700InitalizationComplete]
;     SPC700 is initialized now.
;     This will load the first index of AkaoSPCProgramStartAddressTable and pass it to the APU.
;     This value is the start address of the SPC700 program code on the APU.
;     Writing the value 0xCC to [APUIO0] puts the APU boot loader into memory write mode, starting at the provided address.
C3/0083:	A20000          LDX #$0000      ;Setting the SPC700s program write address.
C3/0086:	AF1400C3        LDA $C30014     ;Load the low byte of first index of AkaoSPCProgramStartAddressTable into Accumulator.
C3/008A:	8D4221          STA $2142       ;Store the SPC700 program code start address into [APUIO2]. (This value is 0x00)
C3/008D:	AF1500C3        LDA $C30015     ;Load the high byte of first index of AkaoSPCProgramStartAddressTable into Accumulator.
C3/0091:	8D4321          STA $2143       ;Store the SPC700 program code start address into [APUIO3]. (This value is 0x02)
C3/0094:	A9CC            LDA #$CC        ;Load 0xCC into the Accumulator. This is the signal command to put the APU into memory write mode at the given address.
C3/0096:	8D4121          STA $2141       ;Store 0xCC into [APUIO1]. This puts the boot-loader into write mode instead of run mode.
C3/0099:	8D4021          STA $2140       ;Store 0xCC into [APUIO0]. This sets the start address and preps the boot-loader to start writing memory there.

[WaitForSPC700SetStartAddressSignal]
C3/009C:	CD4021          CMP $2140       ;Is [APUIO0] set to 0xCC? (APU boot-loader will do so when its done setting the start address)
C3/009F:	D0FB            BNE $009C       ;Branch to WaitForSPC700SetStartAddressSignal and continue waiting until the APU responds.

[StartAkaoProgramLoad]
C3/00A1:	A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into the Accumulator.
C3/00A3:	EB              XBA             ;Switch A & B Accumulators.
C3/00A4:	BF0800C3        LDA $C30008,X   ;Load the low byte of [AkaoSPCProgramPointerTable, X] into the Accumulator.
C3/00A8:	8514            STA $14         ;Store the value into [LOAD_AKAO_PROGRAM_ADDRESS (Low)].
C3/00AA:	BF0900C3        LDA $C30009,X   ;Load the high byte of [AkaoSPCProgramPointerTable, X] into the Accumulator.
C3/00AE:	8515            STA $15         ;Store the value into [LOAD_AKAO_PROGRAM_ADDRESS (High)].
C3/00B0:	A9C3            LDA #$C3        ;All initialization programs are located in this bank.
C3/00B2:	8516            STA $16         ;Store 0xC3 into [LOAD_AKAO_PROGRAM_ADDRESS (Bank)].
C3/00B4:	A00000          LDY #$0000      ;Load 0x0000 into Register Y.
C3/00B7:	B714            LDA [$14],Y     ;First byte is the low byte size of the program in bytes.
C3/00B9:	18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
C3/00BA:	6902            ADC #$02        ;Add two to the low byte of the size.
C3/00BC:	8510            STA $10         ;Store the result into [AKAO_PROGRAM_SIZE (Low)].
C3/00BE:	C8              INY             ;Increment Register Y.
C3/00BF:	B714            LDA [$14],Y     ;Second byte is the high byte size of the program in bytes.
C3/00C1:	6900            ADC #$00        ;Add the carry flag to the high byte.
C3/00C3:	8511            STA $11         ;Store the result into [AKAO_PROGRAM_SIZE (High)].
C3/00C5:	C8              INY             ;Increment Register Y.

[LoadAkaoProgram]
C3/00C6:	B714            LDA [$14],Y     ;Load the next byte from [LOAD_AKAO_PROGRAM_ADDRESS, Y] into the Accumulator.
C3/00C8:	8D4121          STA $2141       ;Store the byte into [APUIO1].
C3/00CB:	EB              XBA             ;High byte of A is the destination offset (from the start address) for the byte written into [APUIO1].
C3/00CC:	8D4021          STA $2140       ;Store the offset [APUIO0].
C3/00CF:	CD4021          CMP $2140       ;The SPC700 boot-loader responds with the destination offset in [APUIO0] when the write is complete.
C3/00D2:	D0FB            BNE $00CFF      ;Wait for the write to complete.
C3/00D4:	1A              INC A           ;Move to the next the destination offset.
C3/00D5:	EB              XBA             ;Move the destination offset back to the high byte of the Accumulator.
C3/00D6:	C8              INY             ;Increment the program loader index.
C3/00D7:	C410            CPY $10         ;Compare the program index against [AKAO_PROGRAM_SIZE].
C3/00D9:	D0EB            BNE $00C6       ;Branch back to LoadAkaoProgram if ProgramLoaderIndex < [AKAO_PROGRAM_SIZE].

[PrepareNextAkaoProgamLoad]
C3/00DB:	EB              XBA             ;Switch back to the destination offset part of the Accumulator.
C3/00DC:	1A              INC A           ;To set a new start address to the boot-loader a value at least two higher then 
C3/00DD:	1A              INC A           ;the previously written value must be written to [APUIO0].
C3/00DE:	1A              INC A           ;The standard way to do this is to increment the destination address by three.
C3/00DF:	D001            BNE $00E2       ;Zero cannot be used because it will cause the boot-loader to exist and start running.
C3/00E1:	1A              INC A           ;So if an increment by three would be zero, then increment an extra time.
C3/00E2:	E8              INX             ;Increment the Program Index.
C3/00E3:	E8              INX             ;Increment the Program Index again to move to the next set in the tables.
C3/00E4:	E00C00          CPX #$000C      ;Compare the Program Index again 0x000C. (6 Total Programs to be loaded)
C3/00E7:	F01D            BEQ $0106       ;If we are done loading programs then branch to StartAkaoEngine.

[SetStartAddressForNextAkaoProgamLoad]
C3/00E9:	EB              XBA             ;Move the signal byte back to the B-Accumulator.
C3/00EA:	BF1400C3        LDA $C30014,X   ;Load the low byte of next index of AkaoSPCProgramStartAddressTable into Accumulator.
C3/00EE:	8D4221          STA $2142       ;Store the low byte of the program start address into [APUIO2].
C3/00F1:	BF1500C3        LDA $C30015,X   ;Load the high byte of next index of AkaoSPCProgramStartAddressTable into Accumulator.
C3/00F5:	8D4321          STA $2143       ;Store the high byte of the program start address into [APUIO3].
C3/00F8:	EB              XBA             ;Move the signal byte back to the A-Accumulator.
C3/00F9:	8D4121          STA $2141       ;Store the signal into [APUIO1].
C3/00FC:	8D4021          STA $2140       ;Store the signal into [APUIO0].

[WaitForWriteStartAddressUpdate]
C3/00FF:	CD4021          CMP $2140       ;SPC700 boot-loader responds with the signal byte that was sent when the write start address update is complete.
C3/0102:	D0FB            BNE $00FF       ;Branch to WaitForWriteStartAddressUpdate and spin until the APU responds.
C3/0104:	809B            BRA $00A1       ;Branch to StartAkaoProgramLoad and load the next program into the APU.

[StartAkaoEngine]
C3/0106:	A00002          LDY #$0200      ;Load the address that the SPC700 should start code execution at into Register Y.
C3/0109:	8C4221          STY $2142       ;Store code execution start address into [APUIO2] & [APUIO3].
C3/010C:	EB              XBA             ;Move the signal byte back to the B-Accumulator.
C3/010D:	A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into the A-Accumulator.
C3/010F:	8D4121          STA $2141       ;Store 0x00 into [APUIO1]. Signal the APU to prepare for code execution.
C3/0112:	EB              XBA             ;Move the signal byte back to the A-Accumulator.
C3/0113:	8D4021          STA $2140       ;Store the signal byte in [APUIO0].

[WaitForAkaoEngineStart]
C3/0116:	CD4021          CMP $2140       ;Right before the boot-loader starts code execution it will write the signal byte to [APUIO0].
C3/0119:	D0FB            BNE $0116       ;Branch to WaitForAkaoEngineStart and spin until the APU responds.
C3/011B:	EB              XBA             ;Move the 0x00 back to the B-Accumulator.
C3/011C:	8D4021          STA $2140       ;Store 0x00 into [APUIO0]. AkaoEngine is idle and has no active command.
C3/011F:	A20001          LDX #$0100      ;Load 0x0100 into Register X. (Size of the APU Memory Page)

[ClearAPUMemoryPage]
C3/0122:	9DFF1D          STA $1DFF,X     ;Fill $1E00-$1EFF with zeroes.
C3/0125:	CA              DEX             ;Decrement Register X.
C3/0126:	D0FA            BNE $0122       ;Branch to ClearAPUMemoryPage until the whole page has been cleared.
C3/0128:	A9FF            LDA #$FF        ;Load 0xFF into the Accumulator.
C3/012A:	8505            STA $05         ;[00/1E05]
C3/012C:	C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/012E:	AFE21AC3        LDA $C31AE2     ;Load the block size of SoundFXBitRateReductionSampleData.
C3/0132:	18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
C3/0133:	690048          ADC #$4800      ;0x4DF1
C3/0136:	85F8            STA $F8         ;[00/1EF8]
C3/0138:	8548            STA $48         ;[00/1E48]
C3/013A:	A20008          LDX #$0800      ;Load 0x0800 into Register X. (Number of SpinWait iterations)

[AkaoEngineInitializeSpinWait]
;     This is doing nothing but burning cycles to allow the AkaoEngine to catch up and initialize itself.
C3/013D:	CA              DEX             ;Decrement Register X.
C3/013E:	D0FD            BNE $013D       ;Branch to AkaoEngineInitializeSpinWait until Register X hits zero.

[]
C3/0140:	E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/0142:	A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into the Accumulator.
C3/0144:	85FA            STA $FA         ;[00/1EFA]
C3/0146:	A9C3            LDA #$C3        ;Bank byte?
C3/0148:	85FB            STA $FB         ;[00/1EFB]
C3/014A:	A902            LDA #$02        ;Load 0x02 into the Accumulator.
C3/014C:	85F0            STA $F0         ;[00/1EF0]
C3/014E:	D001            BNE $0151       ;Seems to be a guard against A being zero, but we loaded a hardcoded 0x02.
C3/0150:	1A              INC A
C3/0151:	0A              ASL A
C3/0152:	0A              ASL A
C3/0153:	0A              ASL A           ;Shift 0x02 left three times and you get 0x10...
C3/0154:	49FF            EOR #$FF        ;Inverting 0x10 gives you 0xEF...
C3/0156:	1A              INC A           ;Now 0xF0....
C3/0157:	18              CLC 
C3/0158:	69FA            ADC #$FA        ;Adding 0xFA gives you 0xEA.
C3/015A:	85F2            STA $F2         ;[00/1EF2]
C3/015C:	64F1            STZ $F1         ;[00/1EF1]
C3/015E:	802D            BRA $018D       ;Branch to CleanStackAndReturn.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[RunAkaoCommand]
C3/0160:	8B              PHB             ;Push Data Bank Register.
C3/0161:	0B              PHD             ;Push Direct Page Register.
C3/0162:	08              PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
C3/0163:	C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/0165:	C210            REP #$10        ;Enable 16-Bit Index Registers.
C3/0167:	48              PHA             ;Push Accumulator.
C3/0168:	DA              PHX             ;Push Register X.
C3/0169:	5A              PHY             ;Push Register Y.
C3/016A:	E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/016C:	A900            LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
C3/016E:	48              PHA             ;Push 0x00 onto the stack.
C3/016F:	AB              PLB             ;Pull Data Bank Register.
C3/0170:	A2001E          LDX #$1E00      ;Load 0x1E00 into Register X.
C3/0173:	DA              PHX             ;Push 0x1E00 into the stack.
C3/0174:	2B              PLD             ;Pull 0x1E00 Direct Page Register.

[RunAkaoCommandCore]
C3/0175:	E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/0177:	A500            LDA $00         ;Load [AKAO_COMMAND] into Accumulator.
C3/0179:	6400            STZ $00         ;Store 0x00 into [AKAO_COMMAND].
C3/017B:	F010            BEQ $018D       ;If the command byte is zero then branch to CleanStackAndReturn.
C3/017D:	3008            BMI $0187       ;Branch if the command byte is greater than 0x7F.
C3/017F:	C901            CMP #$01        ;Test to see if the command is 0x01.
C3/0181:	F015            BEQ $0198       ;Branch if it was.
C3/0183:	C970            CMP #$70        ;Test to see if the command is greater or equal to 0x70.
C3/0185:	B003            BCS $018A	    ;Branch if it was greater then that.

[Command >= 0x80 | Command <= 0x6F]
C3/0187:	4C9F05          JMP $059F       ;

[Command >= 0x70 & Command <= 0x7F]
C3/018A:	4CF305          JMP $05F3       ;

[CleanStackAndReturn]
C3/018D:	C220            REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/018F:	C210            REP #$10        ;Enable 16-Bit Index Registers.
C3/0191:	7A              PLY             ;Pull Register X.
C3/0192:	FA              PLX             ;Pull Register Y.
C3/0193:	68              PLA             ;Pull Accumulator.
C3/0194:	28              PLP             ;Pull Processor Status Register.
C3/0195:	2B              PLD             ;Pull Direct Page Register.
C3/0196:	AB              PLB             ;Pull Data Bank Register.
C3/0197:	6B              RTL             ;Return Long.

[Command == 0x01]
; Startup commands from C7 RAM block.
; 01 - Command Type
; 2D - Table Index
; 0B - Flags? (Lower Nibble == Run SPC Command 83)
; 0F - Passed to APU
; 01 2C 0F 0F
C3/0198:	E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
C3/019A:	EB              XBA             ;Move the command byte to the B-Accumulator.
C3/019B:	A502            LDA $02		    ;Load [AKAO_ARGUMENT2] into Accumulator.
C3/019D:	290F            AND #$0F        ;Remove the high nibble of [AKAO_ARGUMENT2].
C3/019F:	48              PHA             ;Push the formatted argument onto the stack.
C3/01A0:	A501            LDA $01		    ;Load [AKAO_ARGUMENT1] into Accumulator.
C3/01A2:	48              PHA             ;Push [AKAO_ARGUMENT1] into the stack.
C3/01A3:	FA              PLX             ;Pull {(([AKAO_ARGUMENT2] & 0x0F) << 16) | [AKAO_ARGUMENT1]} into Register X.
C3/01A4:	E405            CPX $05		    ;[00/1E05]-[00/1E06]

[AkaoSongSequencePointerTable]
;     This table is the games sound track. There are 58 unique tracks, along with 4 duplicates.
;     The pointers in this table point to the AkaoSequence song data for the track.
;     Track names are taken from the 2018 Secret of Mana Original Soundtrack for the remake.
;     The 2018 OST provides track names to several tracks here that previously did not have offical names.
;     'Menu' is now called 'The Door into the World'.
;     All of the 'Thing Get' Fanfares now have offical (And utterly terrible) names. Victory! and Victory!! as names? And one is a 'Thing Get' track.
;     Several tracks do not have official names however and only exist in game, so their track names will be made up by me.
;     Track 18: BossDefeated       - This is the track that plays while a boss is exploding.
;     Track 19: CannonTravelLaunch - This track is the sound of the Cannon Travel cannon firing.
;     Track 1A: CannonTravelFlight - This track is the song that plays during the Mode 7 overworld transition when using Cannon Travel.
;     Track 1E: BurningCastle      - This track plays during the castle rooftop events after defeating Mech Rider 2.
;     Track 21: UnusedFanfare01    - An unused Fanfare track. Loud and leads with trumpets.
;     Track 23: UnusedFanfare02    - An unused Fanfare track. Leads with snare rolls.
;     Track 28: FlammieDescends    - This track is played while Flammie is picking up the PC after using the Flammie Drum.
;     Track 2D: OtherworldlyHowl   - This is the track that plays when the game first turns on, during the Squaresoft logo splash screen.
